<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center; text-indent: -0.25in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000483" name="us-gaap:OrganizationConsolidationAndPresentationOfFinancialStatementsDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_804_eus-gaap--OrganizationConsolidationAndPresentationOfFinancialStatementsDisclosureTextBlock_zEHIkcCEKrA8" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>1.
<span id="xdx_827_zsIjDI2hDKfl">Organization and Business Overview</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Biofrontera
Inc., a Delaware corporation (the &#8220;Company,&#8221; &#8220;we,&#8221; &#8220;us,&#8221; &#8220;our,&#8221; or &#8220;Biofrontera&#8221;),
is a United States based biopharmaceutical company engaging in the development, manufacturing, and commercialization of pharmaceutical
products for the treatment of dermatological conditions with a focus on photodynamic therapy (&#8220;PDT&#8221;). The Company&#8217;s
products, which include Ameluz<sup>&#174;</sup> as well as the BF-RhodoLED<sup>&#174;</sup> and RhodoLED<sup>&#174;</sup> XL lamp series
(together, the &#8220;RhodoLED<sup>&#174;</sup> Lamps&#8221;), are used for the treatment of actinic keratosis (&#8220;AK&#8221;), a common
skin condition characterized by the growth of pre-cancerous skin lesions (or &#8220;AKs&#8221;). With our national commercial team, we
generate revenue by selling our products directly to dermatology offices and groups.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Effective
June 1, 2024, we assumed control of all clinical trials relating to Ameluz in the United States through Biofrontera Discovery GmbH (&#8220;Discovery&#8221;),
our wholly owned subsidiary organized under the laws of Germany and formed in February 2022. Our research and development (&#8220;R&amp;D&#8221;)
programs are focused on label expansion for Ameluz as well as supporting PDT growth by improving the capabilities of the RhodoLED Lamps
to better fulfill the needs of dermatologists.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
October 20, 2025, the Company entered into (i) an Asset Purchase Agreement (the &#8220;Transfer Agreement&#8221;) and (ii) an Earnout Agreement
(together with the Transfer Agreement, the &#8220;Agreements&#8221;) with Biofrontera AG and its consolidated subsidiaries (the &#8220;Biofrontera
Group&#8221;), pursuant to which the Company acquired all rights in the United States (the &#8220;U.S. Rights&#8221;) to Ameluz and RhodoLED
(the &#8220;Strategic Transaction&#8221;). In exchange for the U.S. Rights, and in addition to a monthly earnout payable to the Biofrontera
Group and the Company&#8217;s assumption of all costs associated with the U.S. business, Biofrontera AG received <span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--SharesIssued_iI_pid_c20251020__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--BiofronteraGroupMember__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__custom--SeriesDConvertiblePreferredStockMember_zAo9zO4XPq6i" title="Shares issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssued" contextRef="AsOf2025-10-20_custom_BiofronteraGroupMember_custom_SeriesDConvertiblePreferredStockMember" id="Fact000485" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">3,019</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of Series D Convertible
Preferred Stock, par value $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--PreferredStockParOrStatedValuePerShare_iI_pid_c20251020__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--BiofronteraGroupMember__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__custom--SeriesDConvertiblePreferredStockMember_zgIDdmcpTmT6" title="Preferred stock, par value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PreferredStockParOrStatedValuePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2025-10-20_custom_BiofronteraGroupMember_custom_SeriesDConvertiblePreferredStockMember" id="Fact000487" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">0.001</ix:nonFraction></span> per share (the &#8220;Series D Preferred Stock&#8221;). The Strategic Transaction was funded through
an $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--Investments_iI_pn5n6_c20251020__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__us-gaap--InvestorMember_zWS8PIaXgkx2" title="Investment"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Investments" contextRef="AsOf2025-10-20_us-gaap_InvestorMember" id="Fact000489" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">11.0</ix:nonFraction></span> million investment by existing investors. See Note 12. Related Party Transactions for additional
information.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
November 6, 2025, the Company completed the sale of the long-lived intangible asset relating to its Xepi product line. The Company
received fixed consideration in the amount of $<span id="xdx_90D_ecustom--AssetAcquisitionConsiderationTransferredContingentConsiderationFixed_pn5n6_c20251106__20251106_zehOr58Zigug" title="Contingent consideration, fixed"><ix:nonFraction name="BFRI:AssetAcquisitionConsiderationTransferredContingentConsiderationFixed" contextRef="From2025-11-062025-11-06" id="Fact000491" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">3.0</ix:nonFraction></span> million and may receive up to $<span id="xdx_90C_ecustom--AssetAcquisitionConsiderationTransferredContingentConsiderationVariable_pn5n6_c20251106__20251106__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MaximumMember_zSb888WcNWb7" title="Contingent consideration, variable"><ix:nonFraction name="BFRI:AssetAcquisitionConsiderationTransferredContingentConsiderationVariable" contextRef="From2025-11-062025-11-06_srt_MaximumMember" id="Fact000493" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">7.0</ix:nonFraction></span>&#160;million of variable consideration
contingent upon the buyer&#8217;s future activities See Note 10. Asset Held for Sale in the Company&#8217;s Annual Report on Form
10-K for the year ended December 31, 2025 (the &#8220;2025 Form 10-K&#8221;) for additional information.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Liquidity
and Going Concern</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
accompanying financial statements have been prepared on a going concern basis, which contemplates the realization of assets and satisfaction
of liabilities in the ordinary course of business. Since we commenced operations in 2015, we have generated significant losses. The Company
incurred net cash outflows from operations of $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--NetCashProvidedByUsedInOperatingActivities_iN_pn5n6_di_c20260101__20260331_zb3O9eK6Kkri"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NetCashProvidedByUsedInOperatingActivities" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000494" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" sign="-" unitRef="USD">0.1</ix:nonFraction></span></span>
<span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">million and $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--NetCashProvidedByUsedInOperatingActivities_iN_pn5n6_di_c20250101__20250331_zZ9s8k3c7Nbl"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NetCashProvidedByUsedInOperatingActivities" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000495" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" sign="-" unitRef="USD">4.1</ix:nonFraction></span></span>
<span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">million for the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025,
respectively. As of March 31, 2026, the Company&#8217;s accumulated deficit was $133</span> million. The Company&#8217;s primary sources
of liquidity are its cash collected from the sales of its products and cash flows from financing transactions. As of March 31, 2026,
we had cash and cash equivalents of $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--CashAndCashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zCJH6VvMbul6"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashAndCashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000496" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">6.3</ix:nonFraction></span>
million, compared to $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--CashAndCashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_zHhplkLlkFaf"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashAndCashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000497" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">6.4</ix:nonFraction></span> million
as of December 31, 2025.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company cannot provide assurance that it will ultimately achieve profitable operations and become operating cash flow positive or raise
additional debt or equity capital. Additionally, the current capital resources are not adequate to continue operating and maintaining
the business strategy for a period of twelve months from the issuance date of this report. Management believes that these conditions
raise substantial doubt about the Company&#8217;s ability to continue as a going concern for at least twelve months from the issuance
date of this Quarterly Report on Form 10-Q.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company plans to address the conditions that raise substantial doubt regarding its ability to continue as a going concern by, among other
things, continuing to expand the commercialization of Ameluz in the United States while controlling expenses, pursuing the realization
of an additional $<span id="xdx_907_ecustom--SalesMilestonePaymentsAmount_iI_pn5n6_c20260331__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_ze2CBWwUF301" title="Milestone payment"><ix:nonFraction name="BFRI:SalesMilestonePaymentsAmount" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000499" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1.0</ix:nonFraction></span> million in milestone payments from the sale of the Xepi intangible asset and, if necessary, securing additional
capital through equity or debt financings. However, there can be no assurance that the Company will be successful in obtaining sufficient
funding on acceptable terms, if at all. If the Company is unable to raise additional capital when needed, it will not have sufficient
cash resources and liquidity to fund its business operations and may be forced to delay or reduce continued commercialization efforts
or R&amp;D programs which could have a material adverse effect on the Company and its financial statements.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
condensed consolidated financial statements do not include any adjustments to the carrying amounts and classification of assets, liabilities,
and reported expenses that may be necessary if the Company were unable to continue as a going concern.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000501" name="us-gaap:SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80A_eus-gaap--SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock_z9QIq5RM9BA9" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>2.
<span id="xdx_82D_zthz5ixztt78">Summary of Significant Accounting Policies</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000503" name="us-gaap:BasisOfPresentationAndSignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock"><p id="xdx_846_eus-gaap--BasisOfPresentationAndSignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock_zVS7bbwnnBGk" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="xdx_86B_znm52siZrvvi">Basis
for Preparation of the Financial Statements</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
accompanying unaudited interim condensed consolidated financial statements of the Company have been prepared pursuant to the rules and
regulations of the Securities and Exchange Commission (&#8220;SEC&#8221;) for interim financial reporting. Certain information and footnote
disclosures normally included in the annual financial statements prepared in accordance with U.S. generally accepted accounting principles
(&#8220;GAAP&#8221;) have been condensed or omitted pursuant to such rules and regulations. In the Company&#8217;s opinion, the
unaudited condensed consolidated financial statements include all material adjustments, all of which are of a normal and recurring nature,
necessary to present fairly the Company&#8217;s financial position as of March 31, 2026, the Company&#8217;s operating results for the
three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025, and the Company&#8217;s cash flows for the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025. The
accompanying financial information as of December 31, 2025 is derived from audited financial statements. Interim results are not necessarily
indicative of results for a full year. The information included in this Quarterly Report on Form 10-Q should be read in conjunction with
the Company&#8217;s 2025 Form 10-K.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">All
amounts shown in these financial statements and tables are in thousands and amounts in the notes are in millions, except percentages
and per share and share amounts.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">With
the exception of the accounting policies below, there have been no new or material changes to the significant accounting policies discussed
in the Company&#8217;s 2025 Form 10-K.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>&#160;</i></span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000505" name="us-gaap:PriorPeriodReclassificationAdjustmentDescription"><p id="xdx_844_eus-gaap--PriorPeriodReclassificationAdjustmentDescription_zZpkz9gF359h" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span id="xdx_867_zWiuSRTOVnf6">Reclassification
of Prior Year Presentation</span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>&#160;</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Certain
prior period amounts have been reclassified for consistency with the current period presentation. Depreciation expense and
amortization expense previously presented separately in the condensed consolidated statements of cash flows have been combined into
a single line item. The reclassification was limited to the condensed consolidated statements of cash flows and had no impact on the
reported results of operations.&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>&#160;</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>The
Nasdaq Stock Market, LLC (&#8220;Nasdaq&#8221;) Compliance</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
December 31, 2025, the Company received a letter from Nasdaq notifying the Company that the listing of the Common Stock was not in compliance
with Nasdaq Listing Rule 5550(a)(2) (the &#8220;Minimum Bid Price Requirement&#8221;) as the closing bid price of the Common Stock was
less than $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--SharePrice_iI_pid_c20251231__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MinimumMember_zBBThYW5UTg" title="Share price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharePrice" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_srt_MinimumMember" id="Fact000507" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">1.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per share for the previous 34 consecutive business days.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
notice had no impact on the listing or trading of the Company&#8217;s securities on Nasdaq. Under Nasdaq Listing Rule 5810(c)(3)(A),
the Company had a period of 180 calendar days, or until June 30, 2026, to regain compliance with the rule referred to in this paragraph.
The Company has since then regained compliance with Listing Rule 5550(a)(2). See <i>Note 18</i>. <i>Subsequent Events</i> for additional
information.</span></p>
<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>&#160;</b></span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000509" name="us-gaap:UseOfEstimates"><p id="xdx_844_eus-gaap--UseOfEstimates_zWo3XQTkQjok" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="xdx_864_zPL3h5RELF7d">Use
of Estimates</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
preparation of the condensed consolidated financial statements in accordance with GAAP requires the use of estimates
and assumptions by management that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities, as well as disclosure of contingent assets
and liabilities, as reported on the balance sheet date, and the reported amounts of revenues and expenses arising during the
reporting period. The main areas in which assumptions, estimates and the exercising of judgment are appropriate relate to
realization and valuation of receivables and inventory, valuation of warrant liabilities, impairment assessment of intangibles and
other long-lived assets, share-based payments, deferred tax asset valuations, and contingent liability recognition. Estimates are
based on historical experience and other assumptions that are considered appropriate in the circumstances. They are continuously
reviewed but may vary from the actual values.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000511" name="us-gaap:NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_840_eus-gaap--NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock_z2mJf6aLR0f" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="xdx_861_zztZhZUubdr9">Recently
Adopted or Issued Accounting Pronouncements</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>&#160;</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">We
evaluate Accounting Standard Updates (&#8220;ASUs&#8221;) issued by the Financial Accounting Standards Board (&#8220;FASB&#8221;). ASUs not included in our disclosures were assessed and determined to
either be not applicable or are not expected to have a significant impact on our financial statements.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt; background-color: white">In
November 2024, the FASB issued ASU 2024-04, <i>Debt with Conversion and Other Options (Subtopic 470-20); Induced Conversions of Convertible
Debt. </i>This ASU clarifies requirements for determining whether certain settlements of convertible debt instruments, including convertible
debt instruments with cash conversion features or convertible debt instruments that are not currently convertible, should be accounted
for as an induced conversion. It is effective for all entities for annual reporting periods beginning after December 15, 2025, and interim
reporting periods within those annual reporting periods. Early adoption is permitted. The Company adopted this ASU on January 1, 2026,
and the adoption did not have a material impact on its condensed consolidated financial statements and related disclosures.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>


<ix:exclude><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 8; Value: 2 -->
    <div style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="text-align: center; width: 100%"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->8<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td></tr></table></div>
    <div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div>
    <!-- Field: /Page --></ix:exclude>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
July 2025, the FASB issued ASU 2025-05,&#160;<i>Financial Instruments&#8212;Credit Losses (Topic 326): Measurement of Credit Losses for
Accounts Receivable and Contract Assets</i>, which provides a practical expedient for estimating expected credit losses on
current trade receivables and contract assets arising from revenue transactions. This ASU is effective for public business entities for
annual reporting periods beginning after December 15, 2025, with early adoption permitted, and must be applied prospectively. <span style="background-color: white">The
Company adopted this ASU on January 1, 2026, and the adoption did not have a material impact on its condensed consolidated financial
statements and related disclosures.</span>&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt; background-color: white">In
November 2024, the</span> <span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">FASB issued </span><span style="background-color: white">ASU 2024-03,</span> <i>I<span style="background-color: white">ncome
Statement&#8212;Reporting Comprehensive Income&#8212;Expense Disaggregation Disclosures (Subtopic 220-40): Disaggregation of Income Statement
Expense</span></i><span style="background-color: white">.</span> The new guidance requires disaggregated information about certain income
statement expense line items on an annual and interim basis. <span style="background-color: white">This ASU is effective for public business
entities for annual reporting periods beginning after December 15, 2026, and interim reporting periods beginning after December 15, 2027.
The new standard permits early adoption and can be applied prospectively or retrospectively. We are evaluating the effect that this guidance
will have on our consolidated financial statements and related disclosures. </span>&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
September 2025, the FASB issued ASU 2025-07, <i>Derivatives and Hedging (Topic 815) and Revenue from Contracts with Customers (Topic
606)</i>, which provides updates to refine the scope of the guidance on derivatives in <span style="background-color: white">Accounting Standards Codification</span>
(&#8220;ASC&#8221;) 815 and clarify the guidance on share-based
noncash payments from customers in ASC 606. The derivative scope refinement excludes non-exchange-traded contracts with derivative accounting
apart from variables based on market rates, prices and indices, variables based on the price or performance of a financial asset or liability
of one of the parties to a contract, contracts involving the issuer&#8217;s own equity evaluated under ASC 815-40 and call or put options
on debt instruments. The amendments in ASU 2025-07 are effective for annual reporting periods beginning after December 15, 2026, and
interim reporting periods within those annual reporting periods and should be applied either prospectively or on a modified retrospective
basis. We are currently evaluating the effect of adopting ASU 2025-07 on our consolidated financial statements and related disclosures.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
November 2025, the FASB issued ASU 2025-11, <i>Interim Reporting (Topic 270): Narrow-Scope Improvements</i>, which clarifies interim
disclosure requirements. The guidance is effective for the Company&#8217;s interim reporting periods within annual reporting periods
beginning after December 15, 2027. Early adoption is permitted. We are evaluating the effect that this guidance will have on our consolidated
financial statements and related disclosures.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
December 2025, the FASB issued ASU 2025-12, <i>Codification Improvements</i>, which addresses 33 issues, representing amendments
to ASC topics that clarify, correct errors or make minor improvements. The amendments in ASU 2025-12 are effective for annual reporting
periods beginning after December 15, 2026, and interim reporting periods within those annual reporting periods. Early adoption is permitted
in both interim and annual periods in which financial statements have not yet been issued or made available for issuance. If an entity
adopts the amendments in this ASU in an interim period, it must adopt them as of the beginning of the annual period that includes that
interim period. An entity may elect to early adopt the amendments on an issue-by-issue basis. We are currently evaluating the effect
of adopting ASU 2025-12 on our consolidated financial statements and related disclosures.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_858_zyBw1NNwleah" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p>

exercise price to $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1_iI_pid_c20231102__us-gaap--FinancialInstrumentAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyTwoPurchaseWarrantsMember__us-gaap--FairValueByFairValueHierarchyLevelAxis__us-gaap--FairValueInputsLevel3Member_zTigzOLPZci3" title="Class of warrant or right, exercise price of warrants or rights"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" contextRef="AsOf2023-11-02_custom_TwoThousandTwentyTwoPurchaseWarrantsMember_us-gaap_FairValueInputsLevel3Member" id="Fact000540" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">3.55</ix:nonFraction></span> per share (the &#8220;2022 Purchase Warrants&#8221;); (ii) warrants to purchase <span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights_iI_pid_c20220726__us-gaap--FinancialInstrumentAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyTwoInducementWarrantsMember__us-gaap--FairValueByFairValueHierarchyLevelAxis__us-gaap--FairValueInputsLevel3Member_zXSUGM8QA5Ab" title="Class of warrant or right, number of securities called by warrants or rights"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights" contextRef="AsOf2022-07-26_custom_TwoThousandTwentyTwoInducementWarrantsMember_us-gaap_FairValueInputsLevel3Member" id="Fact000542" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">214,286</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of common stock
issued on July 26, 2022, as amended on November 2, 2023 to extend the expiration date until <span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--WarrantsAndRightsOutstandingMaturityDate_iI_c20231102__us-gaap--FairValueByFairValueHierarchyLevelAxis__us-gaap--FairValueInputsLevel3Member__us-gaap--FinancialInstrumentAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyTwoInducementWarrantsMember_zjm5cW29I4Yb" title="Warrants expiration date"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="AsOf2023-11-02_us-gaap_FairValueInputsLevel3Member_custom_TwoThousandTwentyTwoInducementWarrantsMember" format="ixt:datemonthdayyearen" id="Fact000544" name="us-gaap:WarrantsAndRightsOutstandingMaturityDate">November 2, 2028</ix:nonNumeric></span> and revise the exercise
price to $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1_iI_pid_c20231102__us-gaap--FairValueByFairValueHierarchyLevelAxis__us-gaap--FairValueInputsLevel3Member__us-gaap--FinancialInstrumentAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyTwoInducementWarrantsMember_zKrYVSo3oUue" title="Class of warrant or right, exercise price of warrants or rights"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" contextRef="AsOf2023-11-02_us-gaap_FairValueInputsLevel3Member_custom_TwoThousandTwentyTwoInducementWarrantsMember" id="Fact000546" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">3.55</ix:nonFraction></span> per share (the &#8220;2022 Inducement Warrants&#8221;) and (iii) warrants to purchase <span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights_iI_pid_c20231102__us-gaap--FairValueByFairValueHierarchyLevelAxis__us-gaap--FairValueInputsLevel3Member__us-gaap--FinancialInstrumentAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyThreePurchaseWarrantsMember_zTfkXSvOo11f" title="Class of warrant or right, number of securities called by warrants or rights"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights" contextRef="AsOf2023-11-02_us-gaap_FairValueInputsLevel3Member_custom_TwoThousandTwentyThreePurchaseWarrantsMember" id="Fact000548" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,807,500</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of common stock
issued on November 2, 2023 expiring <span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--WarrantsAndRightsOutstandingTerm_iI_c20231102__us-gaap--FairValueByFairValueHierarchyLevelAxis__us-gaap--FairValueInputsLevel3Member__us-gaap--FinancialInstrumentAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyThreePurchaseWarrantsMember_zFvSluhEhQ5e" title="Warrants expiration term"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="AsOf2023-11-02_us-gaap_FairValueInputsLevel3Member_custom_TwoThousandTwentyThreePurchaseWarrantsMember" format="ixt-sec:durwordsen" id="Fact000550" name="us-gaap:WarrantsAndRightsOutstandingTerm">five years</ix:nonNumeric></span> following the date of issuance and with an exercise price of $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1_iI_pid_c20231102__us-gaap--FairValueByFairValueHierarchyLevelAxis__us-gaap--FairValueInputsLevel3Member__us-gaap--FinancialInstrumentAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyThreePurchaseWarrantsMember_zKrX1SHzxm79" title="Class of warrant or right, exercise price of warrants or rights"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" contextRef="AsOf2023-11-02_us-gaap_FairValueInputsLevel3Member_custom_TwoThousandTwentyThreePurchaseWarrantsMember" id="Fact000552" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">3.55</ix:nonFraction></span> per share (the &#8220;2023
Purchase Warrants&#8221;). See Note <i>13. Stockholders&#8217; Equity</i> for additional details.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
2023 Purchase Warrants, the 2022 Inducement Warrants and the 2022 Purchase Warrants were accounted for as liabilities as these warrants
provide for a redemption right in the case of a fundamental transaction which fails the requirement of the indexation guidance under
ASC 815-40. The resulting warrant liabilities are re-measured at each balance sheet date until their exercise or expiration, and any
change in fair value is recognized in the Company&#8217;s condensed consolidated statement of operations. The warrant liabilities are
measured at fair value at inception and on a recurring basis, with changes in fair value presented within the condensed consolidated
statement of operations.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company utilizes a Black-Scholes-Merton (&#8220;BSM&#8221;) model to estimate the fair value of the warrant liabilities which is considered
a Level 3 fair value measurement. Certain inputs utilized in our BSM model may fluctuate in future periods based upon factors which are
outside of the Company&#8217;s control. A significant change in one or more of these inputs used in the calculation of the fair value
may cause a significant change to the fair value of our warrant liabilities which could also result in material non-cash gain or loss
being reported in our condensed consolidated statement of operations.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000587" name="us-gaap:RevenueFromContractWithCustomerTextBlock"><p id="xdx_805_eus-gaap--RevenueFromContractWithCustomerTextBlock_zXkEpKs6RIp7" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>4.
<span id="xdx_829_zj4QoaudqV91">Revenue</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">We
generate revenue primarily through the sales of our products, Ameluz<sup></sup> and BF-RhodoLED<sup></sup> Lamps. Traditional
PDT treatments using a lamp are performed more frequently during the winter. As such our revenue is subject to some seasonality and has
historically been higher during the first and fourth quarters than during the second and third quarters.&#160;&#160;&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;&#160;</span></p>

<span id="xdx_821_z6JtFHgocUkd">Cash Balances and Statement of Cash Flows Reconciliation</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company maintains its cash balances at financial institutions that are insured by the Federal Deposit Insurance Corporation, which at
times may exceed federally insured limits. The Company has not experienced any losses on these accounts and management does not believe
that the Company is exposed to any significant risks with respect to these accounts.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt; background-color: white">Restricted
cash consists primarily of deposits of cash collateral held in accordance with the terms of our corporate credit cards. Long-term restricted
cash was recorded in other assets in the condensed consolidated balance sheet.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000602" name="us-gaap:AccountsAndNontradeReceivableTextBlock"><p id="xdx_808_eus-gaap--AccountsAndNontradeReceivableTextBlock_z4JvSxpnNKll" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>6.
<span id="xdx_820_zH0qbxrtD967">Accounts Receivable, net</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Accounts
receivable are mainly attributable to the sale of Ameluz<sup> </sup>in the United States and are expected to be collected within
twelve months of the balance sheet date. Trade accounts receivable are stated at their net realizable value. The allowance for credit
losses reflects our best estimate of expected credit losses of the receivables determined on the basis of historical experience and current
information. In developing the estimate for expected credit losses, trade accounts receivable are segmented into pools of assets depending
primarily on delinquency status, and reserve percentages are established for each pool of trade accounts receivable.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
determining the reserve percentages for each pool of trade accounts receivable, we considered our historical experience with certain
customers, regulatory and legal environments and other relevant current and future forecasted macroeconomic factors. If we become aware
of any customer-specific factors that impact credit risk, specific allowances for these known troubled accounts are recorded.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
allowance for credit losses was $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--AllowanceForDoubtfulAccountsReceivableCurrent_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zMuZY8nb0Hy8" title="Allowance for credit losses"><span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--AllowanceForDoubtfulAccountsReceivableCurrent_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_zUUtimMF2mxc" title="Allowance for credit losses"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AllowanceForDoubtfulAccountsReceivableCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000604" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AllowanceForDoubtfulAccountsReceivableCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000606" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.1</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> million as of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8AF_zgcjBwAGbbQa" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Our
long-term inventory balance relates to the RhodoLED Lamps. During the three months ended March 31, 2026, the Company recognized an
inventory write-down of $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--InventoryWriteDown_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331_zAJhfiBUaoyj" title="Inventory write-down"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InventoryWriteDown" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000639" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.1</ix:nonFraction></span> million in connection with the U.S. International Trade Commission&#8217;s May 6, 2026 Notice of Final
Determination (See <i>Note 16. Commitments and Contingencies</i>). The write-down was recognized to reduce the carrying value of the
affected inventory to its net realizable value in accordance with ASC 330-10-35 and is presented within cost of revenue-other in the
condensed consolidated statements of operations. <span id="xdx_90A_ecustom--InventoryProvisionForObsolescence_do_c20250101__20250331_zBFSXtp0Ufw1" title="Inventory provision"><ix:nonFraction name="BFRI:InventoryProvisionForObsolescence" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000641" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">No</ix:nonFraction></span>
inventory write-downs or obsolescence reserves were recognized during the three months ended March 31, 2025 that were material to
the condensed consolidated financial statements.&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Intangible

fair value of $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--FinitelivedIntangibleAssetsAcquired1_pn5n6_c20251020__20251020__us-gaap--FiniteLivedIntangibleAssetsByMajorClassAxis__us-gaap--IntellectualPropertyMember__us-gaap--AssetAcquisitionAxis__custom--BiofronteraGroupAcquisitionMember_znavLb9fXoJ9" title="Intangible assets"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:FinitelivedIntangibleAssetsAcquired1" contextRef="From2025-10-202025-10-20_us-gaap_IntellectualPropertyMember_custom_BiofronteraGroupAcquisitionMember" id="Fact000693" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">2.7</ix:nonFraction>
</span></span><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">million and is being amortized on a straight-line
basis over an estimated useful life of <span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--AcquiredFiniteLivedIntangibleAssetsWeightedAverageUsefulLife_dtY_c20251020__20251020__us-gaap--FiniteLivedIntangibleAssetsByMajorClassAxis__us-gaap--IntellectualPropertyMember__us-gaap--AssetAcquisitionAxis__custom--BiofronteraGroupAcquisitionMember_zQpkrMQ9iRE8"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-202025-10-20_us-gaap_IntellectualPropertyMember_custom_BiofronteraGroupAcquisitionMember" format="ixt-sec:duryear" id="Fact000694" name="us-gaap:AcquiredFiniteLivedIntangibleAssetsWeightedAverageUsefulLife">18</ix:nonNumeric></span></span>
<span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">years. Internal use software is amortized over three years.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Amortization
expense was negligible for the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025. Amortization expense is recorded in the condensed
consolidated statement of operations within cost of revenues or selling, general and administrative expense depending on the nature
and use of the underlying intangible asset. <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--ImpairmentOfIntangibleAssetsFinitelived_do_c20250101__20250331_zLJQW2Nq4MVh"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ImpairmentOfIntangibleAssetsFinitelived" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000695" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">No</ix:nonFraction></span></span> <span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">impairment
losses were recognized for intangible assets during the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000738" name="us-gaap:DebtDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80D_eus-gaap--DebtDisclosureTextBlock_zNunScTNPZb4" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>11.
<span id="xdx_82B_zcpLIALPwwnf">Debt</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Convertible
Notes Payable</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>&#160;</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
November 22, 2024, the Company issued $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentFaceAmount_iI_pn5n6_c20241122__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--SeniorSecuredConvertibleNotesMember_zCLJxkvOCbn1" title="Debt instrument face amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" contextRef="AsOf2024-11-22_custom_SeniorSecuredConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact000740" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">4.2</ix:nonFraction></span> million in an aggregate principal amount of the Company&#8217;s Senior Secured Convertible
Notes (the &#8220;Notes&#8221;) pursuant to a Securities Purchase Agreement entered into on November 21, 2024 with its principal stockholders.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Notes bear interest at <span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage_iI_pid_dp_c20241122__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--SeniorSecuredConvertibleNotesMember_zAcFTU3nBBOa" title="Debt instrument interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2024-11-22_custom_SeniorSecuredConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact000742" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">10.0</ix:nonFraction></span>% per annum, payable in-kind (&#8220;PIK interest&#8221;) through the issuance of additional principal on
a quarterly basis. In the Event of Default (as defined in the Notes), the interest will increase to <span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateIncreaseDecrease_pid_dp_c20241122__20241122__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--SeniorSecuredConvertibleNotesMember_z3nuTFc0ZK1f" title="Debt instrument interest rate increase"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateIncreaseDecrease" contextRef="From2024-11-222024-11-22_custom_SeniorSecuredConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact000744" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">15</ix:nonFraction></span>% per annum from the date of written
notice from the holder. The Notes may be converted at any time into shares of the Company&#8217;s common stock at a conversion price
of $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1_iI_pid_c20241122__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--SeniorSecuredConvertibleNotesMember_zDcWGZdIrOvl" title="Debt instrument conversion price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1" contextRef="AsOf2024-11-22_custom_SeniorSecuredConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact000746" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">0.78</ix:nonFraction></span> per share subject to customary adjustments for stock splits, stock dividends and recapitalizations, as described in the Notes.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Notes mature on November 22, 2027, unless earlier converted or repurchased. The Company may not redeem the Notes at its option prior
to maturity. Upon maturity, the Company will pay to the holders of the Notes an amount in cash representing all of the outstanding aggregate
principal amount of the Notes, together with any accrued and unpaid interest. Alternatively, the entire amount of the Notes will be automatically
converted to shares of common stock if the 10-day volume weighted average price of a share of the Company&#8217;s common stock on Nasdaq
is greater than <span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentConvertibleThresholdPercentageOfStockPriceTrigger_pid_dp_uPure_c20241122__20241122__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--ConvertibleNotesPayableMember__srt--ScheduleOfEquityMethodInvestmentEquityMethodInvesteeNameAxis__custom--HolderMember_zrXHkQOcz75g" title="Threshold percentage of stock price trigger"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentConvertibleThresholdPercentageOfStockPriceTrigger" contextRef="From2024-11-222024-11-22_us-gaap_ConvertibleNotesPayableMember_custom_HolderMember" id="Fact000748" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">250</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the conversion price, and certain other conditions are met.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Notes provide for customary events of default and contain conversion limitations, providing that no conversion may be made if the aggregate
number of shares of common stock beneficially owned by the holder would exceed <span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--EquityMethodInvestmentOwnershipPercentage_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20241122__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--ConvertibleNotesPayableMember__srt--ScheduleOfEquityMethodInvestmentEquityMethodInvesteeNameAxis__custom--HolderMember_zOI0FPjVpAP5" title="Stock ownership percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EquityMethodInvestmentOwnershipPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2024-11-22_us-gaap_ConvertibleNotesPayableMember_custom_HolderMember" id="Fact000750" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">9.99</ix:nonFraction></span>% immediately after conversion. There were no events
of default at March 31, 2026.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Notes are secured by substantially all property of the Company, including but not limited to the Company&#8217;s assets, inventory, intellectual
property and accounts.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Notes were accounted for as a liability under ASC 470 and the embedded conversion option has been assessed under ASC 815. Based on the
Company&#8217;s evaluation, there were no embedded features that required bifurcation as a derivative liability.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
connection with the issuance of the Notes, the Company incurred $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--DeferredFinanceCostsNet_iI_pn5n6_c20241122__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--SeniorSecuredConvertibleNotesMember_zJTZJoqpcarc" title="Debt issuance cost"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DeferredFinanceCostsNet" contextRef="AsOf2024-11-22_custom_SeniorSecuredConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact000752" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.2</ix:nonFraction></span> million of debt issuance costs, consisting of legal fees.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">During
the three months ended March 31, 2026, the Company recognized interest expense of approximately $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--InterestExpense_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--ConvertibleNotesPayableMember_zg5AWaeav6Of"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestExpense" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_us-gaap_ConvertibleNotesPayableMember" id="Fact000753" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.1</ix:nonFraction></span>
million and minimal discount amortization. As of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the outstanding balance of the Notes was
$<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--ShorttermDebtAverageOutstandingAmount_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--ConvertibleNotesPayableMember_zRww83fLr5oh"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShorttermDebtAverageOutstandingAmount" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_us-gaap_ConvertibleNotesPayableMember" id="Fact000754" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">4.7</ix:nonFraction></span>
million and $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--ShorttermDebtAverageOutstandingAmount_pn5n6_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--ConvertibleNotesPayableMember_zoZLvzygBD7g"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShorttermDebtAverageOutstandingAmount" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_us-gaap_ConvertibleNotesPayableMember" id="Fact000755" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">4.6</ix:nonFraction> </span>million,
respectively, which is shown net of the remaining unamortized issuance cost of $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--UnamortizedDebtIssuanceExpense_iI_pn5n6_c20260331__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--ConvertibleNotesPayableMember_zydrRJBUT9Ug"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:UnamortizedDebtIssuanceExpense" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_ConvertibleNotesPayableMember" id="Fact000756" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.1</ix:nonFraction></span>
million.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000758" name="us-gaap:RelatedPartyTransactionsDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_806_eus-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsDisclosureTextBlock_zdNL2OQUlIn" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>12.
<span id="xdx_823_z1DQn6Qkxqei">Related Party Transactions</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">We
consider Biofrontera Group to be a related party, as prior to the Strategic Transaction, we relied on the Biofrontera Group as the sole
supplier of Ameluz and the RhodoLED Lamps and following the Strategic Transaction, it is a beneficial owner of more than five percent
of our Series D Convertible Preferred Stock.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">License
and Supply Agreement</span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Under
the Second Amended and Restated License and Supply Agreement (&#8220;Second A&amp;R Ameluz LSA&#8221;), which was applicable for any
tubes purchased through May 31, 2025, the Company had an exclusive, non-transferable license to market and sell its previously
licensed products, Ameluz<sup>&#160;</sup>and RhodoLED Lamps, in the United States and was required to purchase the previously
licensed products exclusively from Biofrontera Pharma GmbH (the &#8220;Former Ameluz Licensor&#8221;). Pursuant to the Second
A&amp;R Ameluz LSA, the price paid was set at twenty-five percent of the anticipated net selling price per unit through 2025 (the
&#8220;Transfer Price&#8221;) that covered the cost of goods, royalties on sales, and services, including all regulatory efforts,
agency fees, pharmacovigilance, and patent administration.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Second A&amp;R Ameluz LSA provided for the transfer of responsibilities for clinical trials relating to Ameluz in the US on June 1, 2024,
including the Company assuming related contracts and transferring key personnel from the Former Ameluz Licensor to the Company.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company entered into a Release of Claims with the Former Ameluz Licensor, dated February 13, 2024, pursuant to which the Company agreed
to release the Former Ameluz Licensor from all claims and liabilities arising out of or relating to any failure by the Former Ameluz
Licensor to perform certain obligations under the Second A&amp;R Ameluz LSA with respect to clinical trials for which the Company assumed
responsibility.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Strategic
Transaction with Biofrontera Group</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>&#160;</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company entered into the Strategic Transaction
on October 20, 2025. See <i>Note 1. Organization and Business Overview</i> for more information.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Pursuant
to the terms of the Agreements, retroactive to June 1, 2025, the Company will pay an earnout of <span id="xdx_904_ecustom--RoyaltyPercentage_pid_dp_uPure_c20251020__20251020__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--BiofronteraGroupMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--PurchaseAgreementMember_zdvKXRzeqRb4" title="Royalty percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="BFRI:RoyaltyPercentage" contextRef="From2025-10-202025-10-20_custom_BiofronteraGroupMember_custom_PurchaseAgreementMember" id="Fact000760" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">12</ix:nonFraction></span>% in years where Ameluz revenues in
the United States are less than $<span><span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--RevenueFromContractWithCustomerExcludingAssessedTax_pn5n6_c20251020__20251020__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--PurchaseAgreementMember__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MaximumMember_zy3qXvJ9dAU2" title="Revenues"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:RevenueFromContractWithCustomerExcludingAssessedTax" contextRef="From2025-10-202025-10-20_custom_PurchaseAgreementMember_srt_MaximumMember" id="Fact000762" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">65.0</ix:nonFraction></span></span> million and an earnout of <span id="xdx_902_ecustom--RoyaltyPercentage_pid_dp_uPure_c20251020__20251020__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--PurchaseAgreementMember_zyDz4cdSsQhk" title="Royalty percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="BFRI:RoyaltyPercentage" contextRef="From2025-10-202025-10-20_custom_PurchaseAgreementMember" id="Fact000764" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">15</ix:nonFraction></span>% in years when Ameluz revenues in the United States exceed $<span><span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--RevenueFromContractWithCustomerExcludingAssessedTax_pn5n6_c20251020__20251020__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--PurchaseAgreementMember__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MinimumMember_zC69P7ldSIkc" title="Revenues"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:RevenueFromContractWithCustomerExcludingAssessedTax" contextRef="From2025-10-202025-10-20_custom_PurchaseAgreementMember_srt_MinimumMember" id="Fact000766" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">65.0</ix:nonFraction></span></span> million,
continuing until the expiration of patent protection on Ameluz. The earnout replaces a transfer pricing model under the now terminated
Second A&amp;R Ameluz LSA. At the acquisition date, the Company evaluated the terms of the earnout arrangement and concluded that the
amount of contingent consideration was not reasonably estimable due to the significant uncertainty associated with the timing and magnitude
of future net sales. Accordingly, no amount related to the earnout was included in the initial measurement of the cost of the acquired
assets. The Company has elected to account for contingent consideration in an asset acquisition as the contingency is resolved (earned
and payable). No contingent consideration liability is recognized for amounts not yet earned.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company also agreed to assume the defense of co-defendant Biofrontera Group and all costs associated therewith in connection with certain
legal actions pending in the United States which will be paid directly to the legal advisors by the Company. Details of the legal claims
are disclosed in <i>Note 16. Commitments and Contingencies &#8211; Legal Claims.</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Effective
as of the date of the Strategic Transaction and for the following three years, as long as Biofrontera AG holds any shares of Series D
Preferred Stock (or shares of common stock that were converted from Series D Preferred Stock), Biofrontera AG shall have the right to
appoint (i) one individual to the Company&#8217;s board of directors if the board consists of seven or fewer members; or (ii) two individuals
to the Company&#8217;s board of directors if the board consists of eight or more directors.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">If
the Company does not achieve the Minimum Order Amount as defined in <i>Note 16. Commitments and Contingencies</i> for two consecutive
calendar years starting on January 1, 2026, then the Biofrontera Group will have the right to terminate the Agreements and recover all
assets transferred to the Company.</span></p>


<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Amounts
Due and Payable</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Amounts
due and payable to Biofrontera Group as of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025 were $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--OtherReceivablesNetCurrent_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zqk8nOfR7ZGf" title="Other receivables"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherReceivablesNetCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000768" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1.3</ix:nonFraction></span> million and $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--OtherReceivablesNetCurrent_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_zmFQfKnirDyj" title="Other receivables"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherReceivablesNetCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000770" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">4.8</ix:nonFraction></span> million, respectively, and
were recorded in accounts payable, related parties and when applicable, net of accounts receivable, in the condensed consolidated balance
sheets. Amounts due from the Biofrontera Group as of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025 were $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--OtherAssetsCurrent_iI_pn5n6_c20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__us-gaap--RelatedPartyMember_zY80SLiIpe3l" title="Other assets"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherAssetsCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_RelatedPartyMember" id="Fact000772" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.7</ix:nonFraction></span> million and $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--OtherAssetsCurrent_iI_pn5n6_c20251231__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__us-gaap--RelatedPartyMember_zqauLVAmTref" title="Other assets"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherAssetsCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_us-gaap_RelatedPartyMember" id="Fact000774" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.7</ix:nonFraction></span> million, respectively,
and recorded as other assets, related party.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Inventory
Purchases</i>&#160;&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Purchases
from the Biofrontera Group of the previously licensed products (inclusive of estimated and actual purchase price adjustments) were $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--CostOfRevenue_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331__srt--ProductOrServiceAxis__us-gaap--LicenseMember_zPxyanJe0kr4" title="Related party costs"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CostOfRevenue" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_us-gaap_LicenseMember" id="Fact000776" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.4</ix:nonFraction></span>
million and $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--CostOfRevenue_pn5n6_c20250101__20250331__srt--ProductOrServiceAxis__us-gaap--LicenseMember_zoXwW72JuTSf" title="Related party costs"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CostOfRevenue" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31_us-gaap_LicenseMember" id="Fact000778" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">3.0</ix:nonFraction></span> million during the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025, respectively.
